It's like a virus. My friend had a glitter-themed birthday party in middle school, and I'm pretty sure there's still glitter in the backseat of my mom's car from when she drove us home. The car is this old silver Honda, and the air conditioning doesn't even work right anymore. It just blows warm air that smells like dust. But yeah, I know what those emails really meant, even if no one else sees it.\n\nDoctor: You mentioned that the emails from your school and clinic were signaling that they didn't want you in healthcare; do you feel like these people are actively trying to harm you or interfere with your life?\n\nPatient: Harm me? Well, yeah, they are definitely interfering. Like I said, since I was in those college classes, I've just known that the school portal and the clinic practicum people were doing it on purpose. They use those little wording changes in the emails and the assignment comments to mark me, to make sure I'm flagged as someone they don't want in healthcare. My mom keeps saying they're just regular messages, but she doesn't get it. \n\nShe doesn't get a lot of things, honestly. Like how she always buys the wrong kind of bread at the grocery store. She gets that heavy wheat bread with all the tiny seeds baked right into the crust. I hate seeds in bread. They get stuck in your teeth and you can't get them out. I had to go to the dentist last year because a popcorn kernel got stuck in my gums for a whole week, and the dentist had to use this sharp little metal hook thing to scrape it out. Dentists are so terrifying. They always ask you questions about your life when both of their hands are literally inside your mouth, which makes zero sense. How are you supposed to answer them? I just kind of grunt. \n\nMy uncle grunts a lot when he watches football on Sundays. He gets so mad at the referees on the TV. He threw a couch cushion at the screen once and knocked over a lamp in the living room. It was a really ugly lamp anyway, it had this weird beaded fringe hanging off the shade. Fringe is so outdated, it looks like something from an old cowboy movie. I don't really like cowboy movies, there's always too much dirt, and horses kind of scare me. Their teeth are way too big, and they look like they're always about to bite you. But um... yeah, they're interfering with my life for sure.\n\nDoctor: When you are not thinking about those emails from school, do you ever find that your thoughts become so disorganized or jumbled that you have trouble carrying out basic daily tasks, like getting dressed or preparing a simple meal?\n\nPatient: Um... disorganized thoughts? I don't know if I'd call it disorganized. I mean, getting dressed is fine, mostly. I usually just grab whatever is on the chair. My mom hates when I leave clothes on the chair, she says it looks messy. But it's my room, so I don't see why it matters. She has this whole thing about keeping the house perfect, like it's a museum or something. \n\nWe went to a museum once on a field trip in middle school. It was the natural history one, with all the dinosaur bones. They had this huge T-Rex skeleton right in the lobby. It's crazy to think things that big used to walk around. I read somewhere that birds are actually related to dinosaurs. Pigeons are basically tiny dinosaurs. There are so many pigeons downtown, they just walk around like they own the sidewalks. Some guy was feeding them bread crusts the other day, and they were all swarming him. It looked like a horror movie. Have you ever seen that old movie with the birds? My dad loves old movies. He has a whole collection of DVDs on a shelf in the basement. The basement always smells kind of damp, like wet cardboard. We had a flood down there a few years ago when the water heater broke, and we had to throw away so much stuff. It was a huge mess. So, um, no, I don't really have trouble getting dressed or making food. I just eat whatever is around.\n\nDoctor: When you are trying to do things like finish a project or manage your daily schedule, do you find that your thoughts become so jumbled or scattered that you simply cannot complete the task?\n\nPatient: Um... finish a project? I don't really have projects anymore since I stopped going to classes. But my schedule... I mean, I don't know about scattered. I just kind of do whatever. Sometimes I sit in the living room and try to watch TV, but the commercials are so loud. Why do they make the commercials louder than the actual show? It's like they're yelling at you to buy things. \n\nMy mom bought this new vacuum cleaner from an infomercial she saw late at night. It's supposed to pick up pet hair, even though we don't even have a pet. I wanted a dog when I was little, a golden retriever, but my dad said he was allergic. I don't think he was actually allergic, I think he just didn't want to walk it in the winter. Winter here is awful, the snow gets all gray and slushy on the sides of the road, and the salt ruins your shoes. I had these really nice black boots last year, and the salt left these white crusty lines all over them. I tried wiping them with a wet cloth, but it just made it worse. They were suede, and you're not supposed to get suede wet. It's such a high-maintenance material. Kind of like velvet. Velvet feels weird if you rub it the wrong way, it gives me the shivers. So, yeah, I don't really know about my thoughts being jumbled for a schedule, I just don't have much to do anyway.\n\nDoctor: You mentioned you finished high school last year; how long have you felt that the school and clinic were sending you these messages and that you've been struggling with your motivation?\n\nPatient: Um... how long? Well, I mean, this whole decline, like, everything getting weird... it's been going on since I started the community college program. That was about two years and two months ago, I think. That's when I first noticed the wording in the emails. But community college is weird anyway. The campus was so spread out, and the parking lot was always full. I had to park in this dirt lot all the way in the back, and when it rained, it turned into this massive mud pit. \n\nMud is so gross, it gets stuck in the treads of your sneakers and you end up tracking it everywhere. My mom yelled at me once because I tracked mud into the kitchen right after she mopped. She uses this lemon-scented floor cleaner, but it doesn't even smell like real lemons. It smells like those cheap yellow hard candies that old people always keep in a glass dish on their coffee table. My grandma had a dish like that, but it was shaped like a swan. Swans are actually really mean birds. People think they're graceful and romantic, but if you get too close to them, they'll hiss at you and chase you. I saw a video online of a swan chasing a guy on a golf course. Golf seems like such a boring sport anyway. You just walk around hitting a tiny ball with a stick. I don't understand why people watch it on TV, it's basically just whispering and grass. So yeah, about two years and two months ago is when it all started."
  }
